# FE-03 — Traceability and Test Plan

2026-11-10 · **0 runtime tests executed trong FE-03**. [Registry](FE-03-Action-Registry.md), [State IDs](FE-03-Screen-State-Matrix.md), [Gaps](FE-03-Readiness-and-Gap-Report.md).

## Test-ID convention và acceptance

Mỗi ActionID có bundleunique: `TC-{ActionID}-P` positive+stateassertion,`-N` negative/error,`-AUTH` authorization nếuactorprotected,`-DUP` pending/duplicate choAPImutation,`-KEY` keyboard/naming/focus chointeractivecontrol. Systemloaders khôngfakekeyboardtest. P2blockedP/N chỉgating/no-call tests dự kiến,không test giả positiveendpointchưacó. Tấtcả testIDs **planned**,không countnhưpass.

Đây là testplan,không testimplementation. Từngrow ghi setup/trigger/assertions theo registry,không chỉ“testbuttonworks”. APIrole lỗi tùyservice401/403/404,khôngassert403mọiIDOR. Mutation successassert204nobody/202pending/201DTOtheocontract;statependingkhôngautoOK. SharedCSScomponent tests cóthểreuse implementation nhưng reportcoverage phải liênkếtIDs tươngứng,không nhân testcounts vìreuse.

## SRS coverage — đầy đủ các ID thực tế

## Implementation status bổ sung FE-07 (2026-11-11)

Đây là evidence mới, không biến1012planned IDs thành runtime counts. [FE-07 tests](FE-07-Test-Results.md), [registry/auth routes](FE-07-Authentication-Implementation.md), [scope/issues](FE-07-Open-Issues.md). Canonical `/auth/*` giữ mail-link contract; account paths thực là `/account/*`, supersede planned private settings route paths cho phạm vi đã implemented.

| SRS scope        | Actual screens                | Action family | Evidence                                                                                 |
| ---------------- | ----------------------------- | ------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| AUTH-01/02/03/04 | Register/Login/Account/logout | AUTH/NAV      | ACC-E01/E05, ACC-U06..09/U14 + real Backend regression                                   |
| AUTH-05          | /account/change-password      | AUTH password | ACC-E01, Backend AUTH-16..17                                                             |
| AUTH-06          | Forgot/Reset                  | AUTH recovery | ACC-E02/E04/E06, real generated mail/token capture, SMTP delivery not_verified           |
| AUTH-07          | Request verification/Verify   | AUTH verify   | ACC-E02, explicit confirmation, renderer capture, provider delivery not_verified         |
| USR-02           | /account/profile              | PROFILE       | ACC-E01/U12; avatar deferred_media                                                       |
| USR-04 partial   | /account/settings             | PROFILE/PREFS | timezone/maxPrepMinutes/recommendationOptIn actual PATCH; advanced selectors/P2 deferred |

Public/profile privacy và FE-06 journeys vẫn là regression; G04/G06/G07/G08/G09/G10/G11/G12/G14 không bị xóa hoặc frontend-hack. No FE-08/09/10 UI tự triển khai.

## FE-08 runtime traceability — 11/11/2026

REC01..03/08: `/my/recipes` (draft-only), `/my/recipes/new`, `/my/recipes/:recipeId/edit`; POST201 UUID key, owner GET/list, full-array PATCH version. ED-U01..18/ED-E01..09 are actual implementation tests, not claims that historical FE03 planned IDs ran. REC04/05/06: explicit publish200→public GET verified→unpublish200→public404. REC07 draft soft delete204 only. REC09 remains blocked_media_delivery; existing references preserved, no upload UI/full Media E2E claim. REC10/11 duplicate/scale remain deferred. G09 runtime enum used with Backend OpenAPI fix outstanding;G10 label/actions enforce actual restrictions. See FE-08-Test-Results.md for final execution evidence and FE-08-report.md for acceptance.
